## 🔍 Cross-Page Patterns: Common HTML Issues

These HTML patterns cause the same accessibility errors across multiple pages. **Fix the pattern once in your codebase to fix it everywhere!**

### 🎯 Top Patterns to Fix (Highest Impact)

#### Pattern 1: Affects 70 page(s) - 70 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [aria-input-field-name](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/aria-input-field-name?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**Issue**: ARIA input fields must have an accessible name

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<div class="choices bbd-translation-form__choices notranslate" data-type="select-one" tabindex="0" role="listbox" aria-haspopup="true" aria-expanded="false">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.choices
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('.choices')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://hackney.gov.uk/health-and-social-care/find-support-services
- https://hackney.gov.uk/health-and-social-care/support-disabled-people/disabilities-impairments-and-accessibility
- https://hackney.gov.uk/housing/find-home/apply-join-housing-register
- https://hackney.gov.uk/health-and-social-care/adult-social-care/help-home/find-help-home
- https://hackney.gov.uk/housing/homelessness/help-if-youre-homeless-or-rough-sleeping/find-somewhere-stay-tonight
- *...and 65 more page(s)*

---

#### Pattern 2: Affects 2 page(s) - 2 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [heading-order](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/heading-order?application=playwright)
**Impact**: moderate
**Issue**: Heading levels should only increase by one

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<h3>Opening times</h3>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
.lgd-contact-details > .lgd-contact__group > h3
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('.lgd-contact-details > .lgd-contact__group > h3')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://hackney.gov.uk/births-deaths-marriages-and-citizenship/deaths/mortuary-services
- https://hackney.gov.uk/council-and-elections/scrutiny-local-decisions/overview-and-scrutiny-annual-reports

---

#### Pattern 3: Affects 2 page(s) - 2 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [aria-prohibited-attr](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/aria-prohibited-attr?application=playwright)
**Impact**: serious
**Issue**: Elements must only use permitted ARIA attributes

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<div class="html5-video-player ytp-hide-controls ytp-exp-bottom-control-flexbox ytp-modern-caption ytp-livebadge-color unstarted-mode ytp-large-width-mode" tabindex="" id="movie_player" data-version="/s/player/7460dd14/player_embed_es6.vflset/en_US/base.js" aria-label="YouTube Video Player">
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
iframe[height="462"]
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('iframe[height="462"]')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:
- https://hackney.gov.uk/libraries-parks-and-leisure/libraries/community-library-service
- https://hackney.gov.uk/libraries-parks-and-leisure/libraries/library-services-children-and-teenagers

---

#### Pattern 4: Affects 2 page(s) - 2 occurrence(s)

**Scanner**: axe
**Rule**: [button-name](https://dequeuniversity.com/rules/axe/4.12/button-name?application=playwright)
**Impact**: critical
**Issue**: Buttons must have discernible text

**HTML Pattern**:
```html
<button class="ytmVideoInfoLink ytmVideoInfoChannelAvatar"><img class="ytCoreImageHost ytmVideoInfoChannelLogo ytCoreImageFillParentHeight ytCoreImageFillParentWidth ytCoreImageContentModeScaleAspectFill" alt="thumbnail-image" style="background-color: transparent;"></button>
```

**XPath** (use in browser DevTools):
```
iframe[height="462"]
```

**How to Replicate**:
1. Open any affected page in your browser
2. Press F12 to open DevTools
3. Go to Console tab
4. Run: `$x('iframe[height="462"]')`
5. The element will be highlighted

**Affected Pages**:

- https://hackney.gov.uk/libraries-parks-and-leisure/libraries/library-services-children-and-teenagers
